# Preserve legacy WW1 crypto bonuses without affecting La Resistance cryptology.
endsieg_ww1_crypto_effects = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_tech = endsieg_ww1_cipher_crypto
					has_tech = endsieg_ww1_teleprinter_crypto
				}
			}
			set_technology = {
				endsieg_ww1_cipher_crypto = 0
				endsieg_ww1_teleprinter_crypto = 0
				popup = no
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_tech = cipher_machine
				NOT = { has_tech = endsieg_ww1_cipher_crypto }
			}
			set_technology = { endsieg_ww1_cipher_crypto = 1 popup = no }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_tech = teleprinter
				NOT = { has_tech = endsieg_ww1_teleprinter_crypto }
			}
			set_technology = { endsieg_ww1_teleprinter_crypto = 1 popup = no }
		}
	}
}

# Invite historical co-belligerents from Britain, never the other way round.
endsieg_join_historical_allies = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { date < 1936.1.1 }
			date < 1946.1.1
			country_exists = ENG
			NOT = { tag = ENG }
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ENG }
			ENG = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = SOV }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		ENG = { add_to_faction = PREV }
	}
}

# Repair inherited Comintern membership once, then enact Turkey's dated war entry.
endsieg_TUR_historical_alignment = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = TUR
			NOT = { date < 1936.1.1 }
			date < 1946.1.1
			is_subject = no
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				date < 1945.2.23
				NOT = { has_country_flag = endsieg_TUR_pre_entry_alignment_checked }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_faction_with = SOV }
				leave_faction = yes
				if = {
					limit = { country_exists = GER has_war_with = GER }
					GER = { white_peace = PREV }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { country_exists = JAP has_war_with = JAP }
					JAP = { white_peace = PREV }
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = endsieg_TUR_pre_entry_alignment_checked
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { date < 1945.2.23 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = endsieg_TUR_historical_allied_entry }
			}
			endsieg_join_historical_allies = yes
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_faction_with = ENG }
				if = {
					limit = {
						country_exists = GER
						GER = { has_capitulated = no }
						NOT = { has_war_with = GER }
					}
					declare_war_on = { target = GER type = annex_everything }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						country_exists = JAP
						JAP = { has_capitulated = no }
						NOT = { has_war_with = JAP }
					}
					declare_war_on = { target = JAP type = annex_everything }
				}
				set_country_flag = endsieg_TUR_historical_allied_entry
			}
		}
	}
}

#This should only be used for AXIS vs ALLIES, or JAPAN vs ALLIES
declare_war_on_all_allies = {
	if = {
		#Make sure we dont declare war on UK a second time
		limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = ENG } }
		log = "[?ROOT.GetName] was NOT at war with ENG"
		declare_war_on = { target = ENG type = annex_everything }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_war = yes }
		ROOT_call_allies_both_sides = yes
	}
}

ROOT_call_allies_both_sides = {
	ROOT_call_all_allies_against_ENG = yes
	ENG_call_all_allies_against_ROOT = yes
}

ROOT_call_all_allies_against_ENG = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = ROOT.allies

		add_to_war = {
			targeted_alliance = ROOT
			enemy = ENG
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = ROOT.subjects

		add_to_war = {
			targeted_alliance = ROOT
			enemy = ENG
		}
	}
}

ENG_call_all_allies_against_ROOT = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
	    array = ENG.subjects

		add_to_war = {
			targeted_alliance = ENG
			enemy = PREV
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
	    array = ENG.faction_members

		add_to_war = {
			targeted_alliance = ENG
			enemy = PREV
		}
	}
}

# Vanilla 1.19 leaf effect used by retained African Union events.
set_african_central_bank_variables = {
	set_variable = { org_africa_central_bank_stability_factor = 0.05}
	set_variable = { org_africa_central_bank_industrial_complex_factor = 0.1}
	set_variable = { org_africa_central_bank_infrastructure_factor = 0.1}



}
indonesia_increase_escalation_1_effect = {
	hidden_effect = { INS = { add_to_variable = { var = INS_war_escalation_level value = 1 tooltip = INS_increase_escalation_1_tt } } }
	hidden_effect = { INS = { clamp_variable = { var = INS_war_escalation_level max = 10 } } }
	custom_effect_tooltip = INS_increase_escalation_1_tt
	indonesia_evaluate_escalation_effect = yes
}



# WWI munitions expansion: real state factories, not permanent off-map industry.
# PREV must be the recipient country when this state-scoped effect is called.
# State flags survive conquest and Russian tag changes, preventing duplicate grants.
endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry = {
        while_loop_effect = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add > 0 }
                check_variable = { arms_factory_level < 20 }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_active } }
                set_variable = { endsieg_ww1_industry_base = arms_factory_level }
                set_state_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_active
            }
            if = {
                limit = { NOT = { free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 } } }
                add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
                add_to_variable = { endsieg_ww1_industry_slots = 1 }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_industry_before = arms_factory_level }
            add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { arms_factory_level > endsieg_ww1_industry_before } }
                add_to_variable = { endsieg_ww1_industry_added = 1 }
                PREV = { set_country_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_mobilized }
            }
            subtract_from_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 1 }
        }
}

endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV
            NOT = { has_state_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_demobilized }
            NOT = { has_state_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_stage_1 }
        }
        endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry = yes
        set_state_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_stage_1
    }
}

endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_2 = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            is_owned_and_controlled_by = PREV
            NOT = { has_state_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_demobilized }
            NOT = { has_state_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_stage_2 }
        }
        endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry = yes
        set_state_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_stage_2
    }
}

# Close only the temporary munitions plants. Existing industry is the lower bound.
# Player-built factories above the recorded addition remain; transferred states
# carry their own ledger, rather than deleting factories from the former capital.
endsieg_ww1_demobilize_state_war_industry = {
    while_loop_effect = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { endsieg_ww1_industry_added > 0 }
            check_variable = { arms_factory_level > endsieg_ww1_industry_base }
        }
        remove_building = { type = arms_factory level = 1 }
        subtract_from_variable = { endsieg_ww1_industry_added = 1 }
    }
    # Already converted or destroyed plants must not be replaced by deleting
    # the original factory base during a later weekly cleanup.
    set_variable = { endsieg_ww1_industry_added = 0 }
    # Emergency building plots can be reused for postwar reconstruction;
    # demobilization grants no civilian factories or off-map industry.
    set_state_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_demobilized
    clr_state_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_active
    clear_variable = endsieg_ww1_industry_base
    clear_variable = endsieg_ww1_industry_added
    clear_variable = endsieg_ww1_industry_slots
}

# Country scope; called after scenario war setup and weekly thereafter.
# A neutral country gets no factories. No later WWII bookmark can enter this system.
endsieg_ww1_update_war_industry = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_start_date < 1919.1.1 }
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { has_war = no NOT = { date < 1936.1.1 } } }
            every_owned_state = {
                limit = { has_state_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_active }
                endsieg_ww1_demobilize_state_war_industry = yes
            }
            if = {
                limit = { has_country_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_mobilized }
                set_country_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_demobilized
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                date < 1936.1.1
                NOT = { has_country_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_demobilized }
                NOT = { has_global_flag = compiegne }
                NOT = { has_global_flag = central_powers_victory }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { tag = FRA }
                # FRA: +16 temporary military factories from 1915.7.1.
                if = {
                    limit = { NOT = { date < 1915.7.1 } }
                    16 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 5 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    24 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 2 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    27 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 2 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    15 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 2 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    19 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 1 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    20 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 1 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    30 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 1 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    31 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 1 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    25 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 1 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                }
                # FRA: +12 temporary military factories from 1917.1.1.
                if = {
                    limit = { NOT = { date < 1917.1.1 } }
                    16 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 3 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_2 = yes }
                    24 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 2 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_2 = yes }
                    27 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 2 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_2 = yes }
                    15 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 1 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_2 = yes }
                    19 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 1 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_2 = yes }
                    20 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 1 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_2 = yes }
                    30 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 1 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_2 = yes }
                    26 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 1 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_2 = yes }
                }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { tag = ENG }
                # ENG: +18 temporary military factories from 1915.7.1.
                if = {
                    limit = { NOT = { date < 1915.7.1 } }
                    126 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 3 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    128 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 5 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    129 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 2 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    130 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 3 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    131 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 2 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    132 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 2 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }
                    125 = { set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 1 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry_stage_1 = yes }

			}
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = SOV_Vladimir_Lenin
			country_leader = {
				ideology=leninism
				traits={ triumphant_revolutionary }
				expire = "1924.1.21"
				id=-1
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { SOV_Vladimir_Lenin = { set_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
		if = { limit = { is_unit_leader = yes } remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
		owner = { if = { limit = { has_idea = SOV_Vladimir_Lenin } deactivate_advisor = SOV_Vladimir_Lenin } }
		if = { limit = { has_ideology = leninism } remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = leninism } }
		hidden_effect = { clr_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited }

		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
		if = { limit = { is_unit_leader = yes } remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
		owner = { if = { limit = { has_idea = mikhail_frinovsky } deactivate_advisor = mikhail_frinovsky } }
		hidden_effect = { clr_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited }
	}
}

endsieg_character_activate_sov_mikhail_kalinin_3622469b = {
	# PREV is the activation country inside the immediate character scope.
	hidden_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character = SOV_mikhail_kalinin }
			SOV = { has_character = SOV_mikhail_kalinin }
		}
		SOV_mikhail_kalinin = { set_nationality = PREV }
	}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
			has_character = SOV_mikhail_kalinin
			SOV_mikhail_kalinin = { NOT = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { SOV_mikhail_kalinin = { set_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
	}
}

endsieg_character_retire_sov_mikhail_kalinin_3622469b = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character = SOV_mikhail_kalinin } }
		SOV_mikhail_kalinin = { endsieg_character_dormant_sov_mikhail_kalinin_3622469b = yes }
	}
}

endsieg_character_activate_sov_mikhail_tukhachevsky_dc1f2784 = {
	# PREV is the activation country inside the immediate character scope.
	hidden_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character = SOV_mikhail_tukhachevsky }
			SOV = { has_character = SOV_mikhail_tukhachevsky }
		}
		SOV_mikhail_tukhachevsky = { set_nationality = PREV }
	}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
			has_character = SOV_mikhail_tukhachevsky
			SOV_mikhail_tukhachevsky = { NOT = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
			}
		}
		add_field_marshal_role = {
			character = SOV_mikhail_tukhachevsky
			traits = { brilliant_strategist career_officer trait_reckless infantry_officer offensive_doctrine }
			skill = 4
			attack_skill = 3
			defense_skill = 4
			planning_skill = 4
			logistics_skill = 2
			legacy_id = 401
			visible = { NOT = { has_character_flag = SOV_exiled_flag } }
		}
		hidden_effect = { SOV_mikhail_tukhachevsky = { set_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
	}
}

endsieg_character_retire_sov_mikhail_tukhachevsky_dc1f2784 = {

	}
}

		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
		if = { limit = { is_unit_leader = yes } remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
		owner = { if = { limit = { has_idea = sergei_rudenko } deactivate_advisor = sergei_rudenko } }
		hidden_effect = { clr_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited }
	}
}

endsieg_character_activate_sov_sidor_kovpak_9441dee4 = {
	# PREV is the activation country inside the immediate character scope.
	hidden_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character = SOV_sidor_kovpak }
			SOV = { has_character = SOV_sidor_kovpak }
		}
		SOV_sidor_kovpak = { set_nationality = PREV }
	}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
			has_character = SOV_sidor_kovpak
			SOV_sidor_kovpak = { NOT = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
			}
		}
		add_corps_commander_role = {
			character = SOV_sidor_kovpak
			traits = { trait_reckless war_hero commando }
			skill = 3
			attack_skill = 2
			defense_skill = 2
			planning_skill = 3
			logistics_skill = 3
			legacy_id = 0
			visible = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = SOV_exiled_flag }
				has_completed_focus = SOV_organization_of_the_partisans
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { SOV_sidor_kovpak = { set_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
	}
}

endsieg_character_retire_sov_sidor_kovpak_9441dee4 = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character = SOV_sidor_kovpak } }
		SOV_sidor_kovpak = { endsieg_character_dormant_sov_sidor_kovpak_9441dee4 = yes }
	}
}

endsieg_character_dormant_sov_sidor_kovpak_9441dee4 = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
		if = { limit = { is_unit_leader = yes } remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
		hidden_effect = { clr_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited }
	}
}

# Resolve character identities only after confirming the owner has that character.
endsieg_retire_scoped_character = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = { owner = { has_character = ALB_Turhan_Permeti } }
				is_character = ALB_Turhan_Permeti
			}
			else = { always = no }
		} }
		endsieg_character_dormant_alb_turhan_permeti_a4997255 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = { owner = { has_character = BLR_Jan_Sierada } }
				is_character = BLR_Jan_Sierada
			}
			else = { always = no }
		} }
		endsieg_character_dormant_blr_jan_sierada_1a7f72e6 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = { owner = { has_character = ENG_neville_chamberlain } }
				is_character = ENG_neville_chamberlain
			}
			else = { always = no }
		} }
		endsieg_character_dormant_eng_neville_chamberlain_e63b8326 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = { owner = { has_character = ENG_stanley_baldwin } }
				is_character = ENG_stanley_baldwin
			}
			else = { always = no }
		} }
		endsieg_character_dormant_eng_stanley_baldwin_ac96798f = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = { owner = { has_character = GER_INT_adolf_hitler_chancellor } }
				is_character = GER_INT_adolf_hitler_chancellor
			}
			else = { always = no }
		} }
		endsieg_character_dormant_ger_int_adolf_hitler_chancellor_d4193394 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = { owner = { has_character = GER_INT_carl_severing } }
				is_character = GER_INT_carl_severing
			}
			else = { always = no }
		} }
		endsieg_character_dormant_ger_int_carl_severing_7a05976c = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = { owner = { has_character = GER_INT_constantin_fehrenbach } }
				is_character = GER_INT_constantin_fehrenbach
			}
			else = { always = no }
		} }
		endsieg_character_dormant_ger_int_constantin_fehrenbach_5e38ec96 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = { owner = { has_character = GER_INT_franz_von_papen } }
				is_character = GER_INT_franz_von_papen
			}
			else = { always = no }
		} }
		endsieg_character_dormant_ger_int_franz_von_papen_e4a1d02f = yes

# Additional logged WW1 runtime actors; original helpers above remain intact.
endsieg_character_activate_eng_hh_asquith_fdf5f164 = {
	# PREV is the activation country inside the immediate character scope.
	hidden_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character = ENG_hh_asquith }
			ENG = { has_character = ENG_hh_asquith }
		}
		ENG_hh_asquith = { set_nationality = PREV }
	}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
			has_character = ENG_hh_asquith
			ENG_hh_asquith = { NOT = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
			}
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = ENG_hh_asquith
			country_leader = {
				ideology=conservatism
				traits={ }
				expire = "1989.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { ENG_hh_asquith = { set_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
	}
}

endsieg_character_retire_eng_hh_asquith_fdf5f164 = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character = ENG_hh_asquith } }
		ENG_hh_asquith = { endsieg_character_dormant_eng_hh_asquith_fdf5f164 = yes }
	}
}

endsieg_character_dormant_eng_hh_asquith_fdf5f164 = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
		if = { limit = { is_unit_leader = yes } remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_ideology = conservatism } remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = conservatism } }
		hidden_effect = { clr_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited }
	}
}

endsieg_character_activate_fr3_alexander_ribot_722021a6 = {
	# PREV is the activation country inside the immediate character scope.
	hidden_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character = FR3_Alexander_Ribot }
			FRA = { has_character = FR3_Alexander_Ribot }
		}
		FR3_Alexander_Ribot = { set_nationality = PREV }
	}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
			has_character = FR3_Alexander_Ribot
			FR3_Alexander_Ribot = { NOT = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
			}
		}
		add_advisor_role = {
			character = FR3_Alexander_Ribot
			advisor = {
				slot = political_advisor
				idea_token = FR3_Alexander_Ribot
				allowed  = {
						original_tag  = FRA
					}
					traits  = {
						laissez_faire_capitalist
					}
			}
			activate = no
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = FR3_Alexander_Ribot
			country_leader = { ideology = liberalism traits = { } expire = "1989.1.1.1" }
		}
		hidden_effect = { FR3_Alexander_Ribot = { set_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
	}
}

endsieg_character_retire_fr3_alexander_ribot_722021a6 = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character = FR3_Alexander_Ribot } }
		FR3_Alexander_Ribot = { endsieg_character_dormant_fr3_alexander_ribot_722021a6 = yes }
	}
}

endsieg_character_dormant_fr3_alexander_ribot_722021a6 = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
		if = { limit = { is_unit_leader = yes } remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_advisor_role = political_advisor } remove_advisor_role = { slot = political_advisor } }
		if = { limit = { has_ideology = liberalism } remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = liberalism } }
		hidden_effect = { clr_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited }
	}
}

# Germany WW1 rework: initialize historic facts without replaying popup rewards.
GE1_initialize_ww1_rework = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = GER
			date > 1909.12.31
			date < 1919.1.1
			NOT = { has_country_flag = GE1_ww1_rework_initialized }
		}
		set_country_flag = GE1_ww1_rework_initialized
		# Later bookmarks already followed the historical operational plan.
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_start_date < 1914.8.1 }
				NOT = { OR = { has_global_flag = schlieffen_plan has_global_flag = rupprecht_plan has_global_flag = kronprinz_plan } }
			}
			set_global_flag = schlieffen_plan
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1918.3.3 } }
			set_global_flag = treaty_of_brest_litovsk
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1912.1.12 } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_event_100_resolved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1912.5.21 } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_event_109_resolved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1913.6.30 } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_event_101_resolved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1914.8.4 } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_event_108_resolved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1916.8.31 } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_event_110_resolved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1916.12.1 } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_event_103_resolved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1916.12.5 } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_event_102_resolved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1917.7.19 } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_event_104_resolved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1918.1.28 } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_event_105_resolved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1918.10.28 } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_event_106_resolved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1918.11.3 } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_event_107_resolved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { date > 1911.11.3 }
			set_global_flag = agadir_crisis
			set_global_flag = GE1_agadir_settlement_resolved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_reichstag_war_credits }
			set_country_flag = GE1_war_credits_approved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_constitutional_compromise }
			set_country_flag = GE1_reichstag_compromise_agreed
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_parliamentary_control }
			set_country_flag = GE1_parliamentary_control_established
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_wartime_administration }
			set_country_flag = GE1_wartime_administration_ready
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_auxiliary_service }
			set_country_flag = GE1_auxiliary_service_enacted
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_stormtroop_tactics }
			set_country_flag = GE1_stormtroop_program_started
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_spring_offensive }
			set_country_flag = GE1_offensive_planning_ready
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_kaiserschlacht }
			set_country_flag = GE1_offensive_planning_ready
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_war_materials }
			set_country_flag = GE1_war_materials_established
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_blockade_adaptation }
			set_country_flag = GE1_blockade_measures_available
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_hindenburg_program }
			set_country_flag = GE1_hindenburg_program_started
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_constitutional_compromise NOT = { has_idea = GE1_reichstag_compromise } }
			add_ideas = GE1_reichstag_compromise
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_P_war_finance_office NOT = { has_idea = GE1_war_bonds_drive } }
			add_ideas = GE1_war_bonds_drive
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_P_press_bureau NOT = { has_idea = GE1_labor_compromise } }
			add_ideas = GE1_labor_compromise
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_stormtroop_tactics NOT = { has_idea = GE1_stormtroop_training } }
			add_ideas = GE1_stormtroop_training
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_war_materials NOT = { has_idea = GE1_war_materials_board } }
			add_ideas = GE1_war_materials_board
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_ersatz_program NOT = { has_idea = GE1_ersatz_industry } }
			add_ideas = GE1_ersatz_industry
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_blockade_adaptation NOT = { has_idea = GE1_blockade_relief } }
			add_ideas = GE1_blockade_relief
		}
		if = {
			limit = { date > 1916.8.30 }
			set_country_flag = GE1_hindenburg_program_started
			add_ideas = GE1_hindenburg_program_spirit
		}
		if = {
			limit = { date > 1916.12.4 }
			set_country_flag = GE1_auxiliary_service_enacted
			add_ideas = GE1_auxiliary_service_spirit
		}
		if = {
			limit = { date > 1917.7.18 }
			set_country_flag = GE1_reichstag_compromise_agreed
			add_ideas = GE1_civilian_cabinet
		}
		if = {
			limit = { date > 1918.3.20 date < 1918.3.22 }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = GE1_strike_wave days = 8 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { date > 1915.1.1 }
			remove_ideas = home_by_christmas
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_shorter_training }
			add_ideas = shorter_training_GE1
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_power_to_military }
			set_global_flag = power_to_military
			remove_ideas = WWI_general_staff
			add_ideas = WWI_general_staff_upgraded
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_schlieffen_plan }
			set_global_flag = schlieffen_plan
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_zimmerman }
			set_global_flag = zimmerman_telegram
		}
		if = {
			limit = { date > 1915.5.7 }
			set_global_flag = lusitania_sunk
		}
		if = {
			limit = { date > 1915.9.1 date < 1917.2.1 }
			clr_global_flag = unrestricted_submarine_warfare
			set_global_flag = GE1_backs_down
			ENG = { remove_ideas = unrestricted_sub_warfare }
			FRA = { remove_ideas = unrestricted_sub_warfare }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { date > 1917.1.31 }
			clr_global_flag = GE1_backs_down
			set_global_flag = GE1_continues_USW
			set_global_flag = GE1_resumes_USW
			set_global_flag = unrestricted_submarine_warfare
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = USA USA = { has_war_with = GER } }
			set_global_flag = USA_joins_entente
			clr_global_flag = USA_stays_neutral
		}
	}
}

# Migrate completed legacy aliases to the rebuilt visible tree without
# completing the replacement focuses and replaying their rewards.
GE1_v2_migrate_ww1_rework = {


	# Current saves can already carry the old initialized flag; synchronize the
	# rebuilt institutions independently so those saves still receive only the
	# corresponding visible-focus state.
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = GE1_ww1_rework_initialized } } GE1_initialize_ww1_rework = yes }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_war_materials has_completed_focus = GE1_I_war_corporations } } set_country_flag = GE1_war_materials_established }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_blockade_adaptation has_completed_focus = GE1_ersatz_program } } set_country_flag = GE1_blockade_measures_available }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_hindenburg_program } set_country_flag = GE1_hindenburg_program_started }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_auxiliary_service } set_country_flag = GE1_auxiliary_service_enacted }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_spring_offensive has_completed_focus = GE1_kaiserschlacht } } set_country_flag = GE1_offensive_planning_ready }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_stormtroop_tactics } set_country_flag = GE1_stormtroop_program_started }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_wartime_administration } set_country_flag = GE1_wartime_administration_ready }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_reichstag_war_credits } set_country_flag = GE1_war_credits_approved }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_constitutional_compromise } set_country_flag = GE1_reichstag_compromise_agreed }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_parliamentary_control } set_country_flag = GE1_parliamentary_control_established }

	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_I_chemical_cartels NOT = { has_idea = GE1_I_chemical_research } } add_ideas = GE1_I_chemical_research }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_I_prewar_export_economy NOT = { has_idea = GE1_I_export_credit_network } } add_ideas = GE1_I_export_credit_network }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_I_rail_freight_coordination  } NOT = { has_idea = GE1_I_rail_freight_priority } } add_ideas = GE1_I_rail_freight_priority }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_I_war_corporations  } NOT = { has_idea = GE1_I_war_contracts } } add_ideas = GE1_I_war_contracts }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_I_food_rations_supply NOT = { has_idea = GE1_I_substitute_materials } } add_ideas = GE1_I_substitute_materials }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_I_output_acceleration  } NOT = { has_idea = GE1_I_output_acceleration } } add_ideas = GE1_I_output_acceleration }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_I_skilled_worker_protection NOT = { has_idea = GE1_I_skilled_worker_protection } } add_ideas = GE1_I_skilled_worker_protection }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_A_general_staff has_completed_focus = GE1_army_effort } NOT = { has_idea = GE1_A_staff_spirit } } add_ideas = GE1_A_staff_spirit }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_A_field_logistics NOT = { has_idea = GE1_A_logistics_spirit } } add_ideas = GE1_A_logistics_spirit }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_A_combined_arms has_completed_focus = GE1_equipment_effort_4 } NOT = { has_idea = GE1_A_fire_support_spirit } } add_ideas = GE1_A_fire_support_spirit }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_A_elastic_defence NOT = { has_idea = GE1_A_defence_spirit } } add_ideas = GE1_A_defence_spirit }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_N_surface_risk_theory has_completed_focus = GE1_kaiser_expands_fleet } NOT = { has_idea = GE1_N_surface_spirit } } add_ideas = GE1_N_surface_spirit }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_N_coastal_defence NOT = { has_idea = GE1_N_screen_spirit } } add_ideas = GE1_N_screen_spirit }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_N_prize_rules has_completed_focus = GE1_submarine_theory } NOT = { has_idea = GE1_N_u_boat_spirit } } add_ideas = GE1_N_u_boat_spirit }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_N_merchant_protection NOT = { has_idea = GE1_N_merchant_spirit } } add_ideas = GE1_N_merchant_spirit }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_P_cross_class_burgfrieden  } NOT = { has_idea = GE1_P_burgfrieden_compromise } } add_ideas = GE1_P_burgfrieden_compromise }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_P_spd_mandate has_completed_focus = GE1_P_responsible_cabinet } NOT = { has_idea = GE1_P_reichstag_oversight } } add_ideas = GE1_P_reichstag_oversight }

	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_D_haldane_mission has_country_flag = GW_D_haldane_agreed NOT = { has_idea = GE1_D_haldane_mission } } add_ideas = GE1_D_haldane_mission }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_D_mitteleuropa_tariff_board NOT = { has_idea = GE1_D_mitteleuropa_trade } } add_ideas = GE1_D_mitteleuropa_trade }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_D_ottoman_economic_mission has_completed_focus = GE1_berlin_baghdad_1 has_completed_focus = GE1_berlin_baghdad_2 has_completed_focus = GE1_berlin_baghdad_3 has_completed_focus = GE1_berlin_baghdad_4 } has_country_flag = GW_D_ottoman_agreed NOT = { has_idea = GE1_D_ottoman_mission } } add_ideas = GE1_D_ottoman_mission }
}

# Staged scripted effects for the pre-1914 Anglo-German / continental route.
# Parent integration target: common/scripted_effects/Endsieg_scripted_effects.txt.

# Germany keeps a German-led faction when one already exists. If Germany is a
# non-leader, its existing members are marked, released, and restored after a
# new German-led faction is created. Britain leaves or dismantles its old
# faction, then its subjects follow Britain into the new faction.
GE1_D_form_anglo_german_faction = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = GER
			GE1_ww1_may_choose_british_bloc = yes
			OR = { has_war = no has_country_flag = GE1_R_AUH_wartime_truce_accepted }
			ENG = { NOT = { has_completed_focus = EN1_stay_neutral } NOT = { has_completed_focus = EN1_back_france } OR = { EN1_R_no_continental_war = yes GER = { has_country_flag = GE1_R_AUH_wartime_truce_accepted } } NOT = { has_country_flag = EN1_R_neutral_policy } NOT = { has_country_flag = EN1_R_entente_policy } }
			country_exists = ENG
			NOT = { has_war_with = ENG }
			ENG = { NOT = { has_war_with = GER } }
			ENG = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			has_country_flag = GE1_D_naval_convention_accepted
			GE1_D_belgian_neutrality_offer_ready = yes

 OR = { has_completed_focus = GE1_D_anglo_german_alliance has_completed_focus = GE1_R_AUH_london_peace }
 OR = { has_global_flag = GE1_D_anglo_german_accepted has_global_flag = GE1_R_AUH_british_truce_accepted has_global_flag = GE1_anglo_german_alliance }
}
		set_country_flag = GE1_ww1_bloc_british

  if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
   every_country = { limit = { is_in_faction_with = GER OR = { tag = RIE tag = FRA is_subject_of = RIE is_subject_of = FRA } } leave_faction = yes }
  }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_faction = yes } }
			set_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
			if = { limit = { has_start_date < 1936.1.1 date < 1936.1.1 } create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ww1_anglo_german name = GE1_anglo_german_faction } }
else = { create_faction = GE1_anglo_german_faction }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_faction_leader = no }
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = GER
					NOT = { tag = GER }
					NOT = { tag = ENG }
					NOT = { tag = AUH }
 NOT = { tag = TUR }
 NOT = { tag = RIE }
 NOT = { tag = FRA }
					NOT = { is_subject_of = AUH }
 NOT = { is_subject_of = TUR }
 NOT = { is_subject_of = RIE }
 NOT = { is_subject_of = FRA }
				}
				set_country_flag = GE1_D_anglo_former_member
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = GER
					NOT = { tag = GER }
					NOT = { tag = ENG }
					NOT = { tag = AUH }
 NOT = { tag = TUR }
 NOT = { tag = RIE }
 NOT = { tag = FRA }
					NOT = { is_subject_of = AUH }
 NOT = { is_subject_of = TUR }
 NOT = { is_subject_of = RIE }
 NOT = { is_subject_of = FRA }
				}
				leave_faction = yes
			}
			leave_faction = yes
			set_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
			if = { limit = { has_start_date < 1936.1.1 date < 1936.1.1 } create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ww1_anglo_german name = GE1_anglo_german_faction } }
else = { create_faction = GE1_anglo_german_faction }
			every_country = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = GE1_D_anglo_former_member }
				# add_to_faction is evaluated from the faction leader's scope.
				GER = { add_to_faction = PREV }
				clr_country_flag = GE1_D_anglo_former_member
			}
		}


  # Britain withdraws; the remaining Entente keeps its faction and members.
  if = { limit = { ENG = { is_in_faction = yes is_faction_leader = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GER } } }
   if = { limit = { FRA = { exists = yes is_in_faction_with = ENG is_subject = no } } FRA = { set_faction_leader = yes } }
   else_if = { limit = { RIE = { exists = yes is_in_faction_with = ENG is_subject = no } } RIE = { set_faction_leader = yes } }
  }
		if = {
			limit = {
				ENG = {
					is_in_faction = yes
					is_faction_leader = yes
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GER }
				}
			}
			ENG = { dismantle_faction = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { ENG = { is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GER } } }
			ENG = { leave_faction = yes }
		}

		# GER is the faction leader; pass the member tag to the effect.
		if = { limit = { ENG = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GER } } } add_to_faction = ENG }
		ENG = { set_country_flag = GE1_anglo_german_alliance }
		# AUH remains independent on the British route. If it was already in a
		# faction, detach it without importing it into the new alliance.





		# The WW1 startup script can leave DER fighting ENG before the player
		# completes the diplomatic route. End only that isolated conflict.
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = DER
				DER = {
					is_in_faction_with = GER
					has_war_with = ENG
				}
			}
			DER = { white_peace = ENG }
		}

		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_subject_of = ENG
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GER }
				NOT = { tag = AUH }
 NOT = { tag = TUR }
 NOT = { tag = RIE }
 NOT = { tag = FRA }
				NOT = { is_subject_of = AUH }
 NOT = { is_subject_of = TUR }
 NOT = { is_subject_of = RIE }
 NOT = { is_subject_of = FRA }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_faction = yes is_faction_leader = yes }
				dismantle_faction = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
				leave_faction = yes
			}
			GER = { add_to_faction = PREV }
		}
if = {
			limit = { ENG = { is_in_faction_with = GER } }
			set_faction_name = GE1_anglo_german_faction
WW1_configure_anglo_german_faction = yes
			set_global_flag = GE1_anglo_german_alliance
			set_country_flag = GE1_anglo_german_alliance
			clr_global_flag = GE1_D_anglo_german_pending
			clr_global_flag = GE1_D_anglo_german_accepted
			clr_global_flag = GE1_D_anglo_german_refused
			add_ideas = GE1_D_anglo_german_compact
			hidden_effect = { GE1_D_activate_naval_convention = yes GE1_D_activate_belgian_neutrality = yes }
			clr_country_flag = GE1_D_naval_convention_offered

 GE1_ww1_detach_central_partners = yes
}
	}
}

# France is always the leader of the continental faction. Existing faction
# members are preserved when a leader faction is replaced, except Britain and
# its subjects, GER, RIE and ITA; those tags have explicit route decisions.
GE1_D_form_continental_faction = {
	if = {
		limit = {
FRA = { FR3_R_may_choose_continental_alignment = yes }

			RIE = { RUS_ww1_may_be_historical_war_target = yes }
			tag = GER
			country_exists = FRA
			country_exists = RIE
			NOT = { has_war_with = FRA }
			NOT = { has_war_with = RIE }
			FRA = { NOT = { has_war_with = GER } NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			RIE = { NOT = { has_war_with = GER } NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			FRA = { NOT = { has_war_with = RIE } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FRA = { is_in_faction = no } }
			FRA = {
				set_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
				if = { limit = { has_start_date < 1936.1.1 date < 1936.1.1 } create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ww1_continental_alliance name = GE1_continental_faction } }
else = { create_faction = GE1_continental_faction }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FRA = { is_faction_leader = yes } }
			FRA = {
					every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_in_faction_with = FRA
						NOT = { tag = FRA }
						NOT = { tag = ENG }
						NOT = { tag = GER }
						NOT = { tag = RIE }
						NOT = { tag = ITA }
						NOT = { is_subject_of = ENG }
					}
					set_country_flag = GE1_D_continental_former_member
				}
				dismantle_faction = yes
				set_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
				if = { limit = { has_start_date < 1936.1.1 date < 1936.1.1 } create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ww1_continental_alliance name = GE1_continental_faction } }
else = { create_faction = GE1_continental_faction }
			}
		}
		else = {
			FRA = {
				leave_faction = yes
				set_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
				if = { limit = { has_start_date < 1936.1.1 date < 1936.1.1 } create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ww1_continental_alliance name = GE1_continental_faction } }
else = { create_faction = GE1_continental_faction }
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { RIE = { is_faction_leader = yes } }
			RIE = {
					every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_in_faction_with = RIE
						NOT = { tag = RIE }
						NOT = { tag = ENG }
						NOT = { tag = GER }
						NOT = { tag = FRA }
						NOT = { tag = ITA }
						NOT = { is_subject_of = ENG }
					}
					set_country_flag = GE1_D_continental_former_member
				}
				dismantle_faction = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { RIE = { is_in_faction = yes } }
			RIE = { leave_faction = yes }
		}
		# FRA is the faction leader; add the Russian member from FRA's scope.
		FRA = { add_to_faction = RIE }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = GE1_D_continental_former_member }
			FRA = { add_to_faction = PREV }
			clr_country_flag = GE1_D_continental_former_member
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				FRA = { is_faction_leader = yes }
				RIE = { is_in_faction_with = FRA }
			}
			set_global_flag = GE1_continental_alliance_active
			FRA = { set_country_flag = GE1_continental_alliance }
			RIE = { set_country_flag = GE1_continental_alliance }
			clr_global_flag = GE1_D_franco_russian_pending
			clr_global_flag = GE1_D_franco_russian_accepted
			clr_global_flag = GE1_D_franco_russian_refused
			add_ideas = GE1_D_continental_coordination
		}
	}
}

# Italy's recipient decision calls this in ITA scope. Existing Italian
# factions are left or dismantled only for Italy itself, and no Italian
# membership is inferred from an opinion or from the continental offer.
GE1_D_add_italy_to_continental = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = ITA
			country_exists = FRA
			has_global_flag = GE1_continental_alliance_active
			NOT = { has_war_with = FRA }
			NOT = { has_war_with = GER }
			FRA = { is_faction_leader = yes NOT = { has_war_with = ITA } }

 has_completed_focus = IT1_ww1_continental_concert
 OR = { is_in_faction = no is_in_faction_with = FRA }
 NOT = { has_country_flag = IT1_ww1_armed_neutrality }
 NOT = { has_country_flag = IT1_ww1_central_alignment }
 NOT = { has_country_flag = IT1_ww1_entente_alignment }
}


		# FRA is the leader and ITA is the accepting member.
		FRA = { add_to_faction = ITA }
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_faction_with = FRA }
			set_country_flag = GE1_continental_alliance
		}
	}
}

# The Anglo-German compact includes a formal Belgian-neutrality plan. Set it
# only after the real faction membership exists so the later Kronprinz focus
# cannot advertise a plan whose diplomatic prerequisite failed.
GE1_D_prepare_kronprinz_plan = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = GER
			has_global_flag = GE1_anglo_german_alliance
			has_country_flag = GE1_D_belgian_neutrality_pledged
			has_start_date < 1914.7.1
			NOT = { has_war = yes }
		}
		clr_global_flag = schlieffen_plan
		clr_global_flag = rupprecht_plan
		set_global_flag = kronprinz_plan
	}
}

# Start the actual war only after both factions and every required recipient
# have agreed. FRA's faction pulls RIE and accepted ITA into the same war.
GE1_D_launch_continental_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
FRA = { FR3_R_may_lead_actual_alliance = yes }

			RIE = { RUS_ww1_may_be_historical_war_target = yes }
			tag = GER
			has_global_flag = GE1_anglo_german_alliance
			has_global_flag = GE1_continental_alliance_active
			ENG = { is_in_faction_with = GER NOT = { has_war_with = GER } }
			FRA = { is_faction_leader = yes }
			RIE = { is_in_faction_with = FRA NOT = { has_war_with = GER } }
			has_global_flag = GE1_D_anglo_german_war_accepted
			has_global_flag = GE1_D_continental_war_accepted
			has_global_flag = GE1_anglo_german_war_entry_agreed
			NOT = { has_war_with = FRA }
			NOT = { has_war_with = RIE }
			OR = {
				ITA = { is_in_faction_with = FRA has_global_flag = GE1_D_italian_war_accepted has_country_flag = GE1_continental_war_entry_agreed NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
				NOT = { ITA = { is_in_faction_with = FRA } }
			}
		}
		GER = { declare_war_on = { target = FRA type = annex_everything } }
		ENG = {
			add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = GER enemy = FRA }
		}
		RIE = {
			add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = FRA enemy = GER }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ITA = { is_in_faction_with = FRA has_global_flag = GE1_D_italian_war_accepted has_country_flag = GE1_continental_war_entry_agreed NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
			ITA = { add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = FRA enemy = GER } }
		}
		set_country_flag = GE1_continental_war_programme
	}
}

# Refusals cancel only the pending war coordination state. The two active
# faction markers and their members remain intact.
GE1_D_clear_war_coordination = {
	clr_global_flag = GE1_D_anglo_german_war_pending
	clr_global_flag = GE1_D_anglo_german_war_accepted
	clr_global_flag = GE1_D_anglo_german_war_refused
	clr_global_flag = GE1_D_continental_war_pending
	clr_global_flag = GE1_D_continental_war_accepted
	clr_global_flag = GE1_D_continental_war_refused
	clr_global_flag = GE1_D_italian_war_pending
	clr_global_flag = GE1_D_italian_war_accepted
	clr_global_flag = GE1_D_italian_war_refused
	clr_global_flag = GE1_anglo_german_war_entry_agreed
	ENG = {
		clr_country_flag = GE1_anglo_german_war_entry_agreed
	}
	ITA = {
		clr_country_flag = GE1_continental_war_entry_agreed
	}
}

        if = { limit = { has_idea = RUS_ww1_kornilov_emergency_rule } remove_ideas = RUS_ww1_kornilov_emergency_rule }
        if = { limit = { has_idea = RUS_ww1_kolchak_executive } remove_ideas = RUS_ww1_kolchak_executive }
}

		}

			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1916.8.31 } NOT = { has_global_flag = compiegne } NOT = { has_global_flag = central_powers_victory } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_RP_third_ohl set_country_flag = GE1_RP_268_resolved add_ideas = GE1_RP_ohl_directive
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1917.4.8 } NOT = { has_global_flag = compiegne } NOT = { has_global_flag = central_powers_victory } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_RP_easter_message set_country_flag = GE1_RP_270_resolved
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1917.7.19 } NOT = { has_global_flag = compiegne } NOT = { has_global_flag = central_powers_victory } }
			set_country_flag = GE1_RP_peace_bloc set_country_flag = GE1_RP_269_resolved add_ideas = GE1_RP_peace_resolution
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = GER has_focus_tree = german_focus_ww1 date < 1936.1.1 NOT = { has_global_flag = compiegne } NOT = { has_global_flag = versailles } NOT = { has_global_flag = central_powers_victory } }

		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_R_liberal_monarchy NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_constitutional_monarchy } } add_ideas = GE1_R_constitutional_monarchy }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_R_soldiers_dependants NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_dependants_relief } } add_ideas = GE1_R_dependants_relief }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_R_womens_war_work NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_women_war_workers } } add_ideas = GE1_R_women_war_workers }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_R_ohl_public_instruction NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_patriotic_instruction } } add_ideas = GE1_R_patriotic_instruction }

		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_R_renewed_reinsurance NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_reinsurance_spirit } } add_ideas = GE1_R_reinsurance_spirit }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_colonial_logistics NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_colonial_supply } } add_ideas = GE1_R_colonial_supply }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_D_submarine_warning_policy NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_prize_rules } } add_ideas = GE1_R_prize_rules }

		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_R_labour_bureaux NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_labour_bureaux_idea } } add_ideas = GE1_R_labour_bureaux_idea }

		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_R_worker_welfare NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_worker_welfare_idea } } add_ideas = GE1_R_worker_welfare_idea }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_R_reconstruction_credit NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_reconstruction_credit_idea } } add_ideas = GE1_R_reconstruction_credit_idea }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_R_demobilisation_conversion NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_reconstruction_credit_idea } } add_ideas = GE1_R_reconstruction_credit_idea }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_army_effort NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_army_reform_spirit } } add_ideas = GE1_R_army_reform_spirit }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_fortify_the_west NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_trench_engineer_spirit } } add_ideas = GE1_R_trench_engineer_spirit }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_R_air_command NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_air_service_spirit } } add_ideas = GE1_R_air_service_spirit }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_N_naval_staff NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_naval_coordination_spirit } } add_ideas = GE1_R_naval_coordination_spirit }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_N_merchant_protection NOT = { has_idea = GE1_R_merchant_routes } } add_ideas = GE1_R_merchant_routes }
	}

	if = { limit = { tag = GER date < 1936.1.1 has_completed_focus = GE1_D_support_austria NOT = { has_global_flag = GE1_anglo_german_alliance } NOT = { has_country_flag = GE1_R_AUH_reorientation } } set_country_flag = GE1_R_alliance_austria }

 if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_R_eastern_neutrality GE1_ww1_may_choose_russian_bloc = yes } if = {
 limit = { RIE = { has_country_flag = RUS_ww1_german_agreement NOT = { has_completed_focus = RUS_entente_diplomacy } } }
 set_country_flag = GE1_RP_russian_neutrality_accepted
 set_global_flag = GE1_RP_russian_neutrality_accepted
} }
 if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = GE1_R_renewed_reinsurance } GE1_ww1_request_russian_bloc = yes }
}

# Germany's staged AUH settlement helpers. Every transfer is guarded by
# ownership and control so foreign-held states are left to their owners.
GE1_R_AUH_clear_pressure_ideas = {
	if = { limit = { has_idea = AUH_ww1_german_commercial_pressure } remove_ideas = AUH_ww1_german_commercial_pressure }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = AUH_ww1_german_local_pressure } remove_ideas = AUH_ww1_german_local_pressure }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = AUH_ww1_german_ultimatum_pressure } remove_ideas = AUH_ww1_german_ultimatum_pressure }
}

# End the German-AUH war and any AUH-subject wars before moving states.  This
# deliberately targets only the parties in this branch; France and Russia are
# left to the existing continental chain.
GE1_R_AUH_whitepeace_settlement = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = GER GER = { has_war_with = AUH } } GER = { white_peace = AUH } }
	every_country = {
		limit = { is_subject_of = AUH has_war_with = GER }
		white_peace = GER
	}
}

	set_country_flag = GE1_R_AUH_partition_complete
	set_global_flag = GE1_R_AUH_managed_partition_complete

	clr_global_flag = GE1_R_AUH_pressure_pending
	clr_global_flag = GE1_R_AUH_negotiation_pending
}

          WW1_HAB_apply_vienna = yes
      }
}

    if = { limit = { has_idea = EN1_R_parliamentary_scrutiny } remove_ideas = EN1_R_parliamentary_scrutiny } if = { limit = { has_idea = EN1_R_cabinet_continuity } remove_ideas = EN1_R_update_tree = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = ENG has_start_date < 1936.1.1 }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = EN1_R_bookmark_initialized } has_focus_tree = british_focus_ww1 }
            # Record past institutions silently: history completion never replays their events.
            if = { limit = { date > 1911.8.17 } set_country_flag = EN1_R_parliament_act }
            if = { limit = { date > 1914.8.3 } set_country_flag = EN1_R_war_prepared set_country_flag = EN1_R_dora }
            set_country_flag = EN1_R_bookmark_initialized
        }
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { has_global_flag = compiegne has_global_flag = central_powers_victory date > 1935.12.31 NOT = { has_focus_tree = british_focus_ww1 } } }
            EN1_R_clear_war_programmes = yes
        }
        # An armistice or an accepted new alliance closes the hostile blockade.
        if = {
            limit = { has_global_flag = north_sea_blockade OR = { NOT = { has_war_with = GER } is_in_faction_with = GER } }
            clr_global_flag = north_sea_blockade
            GER = { if = { limit = { has_idea = blockade } remove_ideas = blockade } }
        }

        # Completed history focuses do not execute rewards; restore their contracts silently.
        if = {
            limit = { has_focus_tree = british_focus_ww1 has_completed_focus = EN1_back_france NOT = { has_global_flag = GE1_anglo_german_alliance } }
            set_country_flag = EN1_R_entente_policy
            set_global_flag = back_france
        }
        if = {
            limit = { has_focus_tree = british_focus_ww1 is_in_faction_with = FRA has_war_with = GER NOT = { has_global_flag = GE1_anglo_german_alliance } }
            set_global_flag = britain_joins_entente
            set_global_flag = britain_joins_war
        }
}
}
EN1_R_join_entente_war = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   WW1_british_entente_entry_allowed = yes
   country_exists = FRA
   FRA = { FR3_R_may_enter_entente = yes NOT = { has_war_with = ENG } OR = { is_in_faction = no is_faction_leader = yes } }
   OR = { is_in_faction = no is_in_faction_with = FRA }

 OR = { has_completed_focus = EN1_join_war WW1_british_historical_belgian_response = yes }

 OR = { WW1_british_historical_belgian_response = yes NOT = { WW1_historical_ai_preparation = yes } NOT = { date < 1914.8.4 } }
}
  if = { limit = { FRA = { is_in_faction = no } } FRA = { set_rule = { can_create_factions = yes } if = { limit = { has_start_date < 1936.1.1 date < 1936.1.1 } create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ww1_entente name = Entente } }
else = { create_faction = Entente } } }
  if = { limit = { is_in_faction = no } FRA = { add_to_faction = ENG } }
  if = { limit = { is_in_faction_with = FRA NOT = { has_war_with = GER } } declare_war_on = { target = GER type = annex_everything } }
  if = { limit = { is_in_faction_with = FRA has_war_with = GER } set_global_flag = britain_joins_entente set_global_flag = britain_joins_war  if = { limit = { WW1_british_historical_belgian_response = yes } set_country_flag = EN1_R_entente_policy set_global_flag = back_france } }
 }
}
EN1_R_invite_american_ally = {
 if = {
  limit = { USA = { US2_R_may_enter_entente_war = yes has_completed_focus = US2_support_entente } has_start_date < 1936.1.1 date < 1936.1.1 has_war_with = GER is_in_faction = yes country_exists = USA USA = { has_war_with = GER is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_country_flag = US2_R_entente_offer_sent } } }
  USA = { set_country_flag = US2_R_entente_offer_sent country_event = { id = ww1_usa.218 days = 1 } }
 }
}
EN1_R_suez_defences = {
    446 = {
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { is_controlled_by = ENG controller = { is_in_faction_with = ENG } controller = { is_subject_of = ENG } } }
            add_building_construction = { type = bunker level = 1 province = 1155 instant_build = yes }
            add_building_construction = { type = bunker level = 1 province = 12049 instant_build = yes }
        }
    }
}

# Preserve the other members of the Entente when France chooses to withdraw.
FR3_R_withdraw_from_military_commitments = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = FRA FR3_R_no_continental_war = yes }
  if = {
   limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
   if = { limit = { country_exists = RIE is_in_faction_with = RIE RIE = { is_subject = no } } RIE = { set_faction_leader = yes } }
   else = { random_other_country = { limit = { is_in_faction_with = FRA NOT = { tag = FRA } is_subject = no } set_faction_leader = yes } }
  }
  if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes }
  else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
  every_country = { limit = { is_subject_of = FRA is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
  clr_country_flag = FR3_R_entente_policy
  clr_country_flag = FR3_R_continental_policy
  clr_global_flag = GE1_continental_alliance_active
  GER = { GE1_D_clear_war_coordination = yes clr_country_flag = GE1_continental_war_programme }
 }
}
FR3_R_conclude_accommodation = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = FRA FR3_R_no_continental_war = yes has_country_flag = FR3_R_german_offer_accepted GER = { has_country_flag = GE1_D_belgian_neutrality_pledged NOT = { has_war_with = FRA } } }
  FR3_R_withdraw_from_military_commitments = yes
  diplomatic_relation = { country = GER relation = non_aggression_pact active = yes }
  set_country_flag = FR3_R_diplomatic_accommodation
  clr_country_flag = FR3_R_accommodation_requested
  clr_country_flag = FR3_R_neutral_policy
  GER = { set_country_flag = FR3_R_french_accommodation_accepted }
  add_timed_idea = { idea = FR3_R_commercial_dialogue_spirit days = 180 }
 }
}
FR3_R_adopt_neutrality = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = FRA FR3_R_no_continental_war = yes }
  FR3_R_withdraw_from_military_commitments = yes
  set_country_flag = FR3_R_neutral_policy
  clr_country_flag = FR3_R_diplomatic_accommodation
  add_timed_idea = { idea = FR3_R_armed_neutrality_spirit days = 180 }
 }
}
FR3_R_update_tree = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = FRA has_start_date < 1936.1.1 }
  if = {
   limit = { has_focus_tree = french_focus_ww1 NOT = { has_country_flag = FR3_R_bookmark_initialized } }
   if = { limit = { date > 1913.8.6 } set_country_flag = FR3_R_three_year_law }
   if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = FR3_entente_diplomacy NOT = { has_country_flag = FR3_R_neutral_policy } NOT = { has_country_flag = FR3_R_diplomatic_accommodation } } set_country_flag = FR3_R_entente_policy }
   if = { limit = { date > 1917.7.1 } set_country_flag = FR3_R_army_restored }
   set_country_flag = FR3_R_bookmark_initialized
  }
  if = { limit = { OR = { has_global_flag = compiegne has_global_flag = central_powers_victory date > 1935.12.31 NOT = { has_focus_tree = french_focus_ww1 } } } FR3_R_clear_war_programmes = yes }

  if = { limit = { has_focus_tree = french_focus_ww1 has_completed_focus = FR3_invite_greece is_in_faction_with = GRE } set_country_flag = FR3_R_greek_offer_accepted }
  if = { limit = { has_focus_tree = french_focus_ww1 has_completed_focus = FR3_invite_italy is_in_faction_with = ITA } set_global_flag = IT_accept_deal_1 }
  if = { limit = { has_focus_tree = french_focus_ww1 has_completed_focus = FR3_invite_romania is_in_faction_with = ROM } set_global_flag = ROM_accept_deal }

  # Historical France opens Agadir itself on 1 July 1911. The focus and
  # fallback share the same pending/resolved contract, so the chain opens once.
  if = {
   limit = {
    has_focus_tree = french_focus_ww1
    has_start_date < 1911.7.1
    is_historical_focus_on = yes
    NOT = { date < 1911.7.1 } date < 1911.7.8
    country_exists = GER NOT = { has_war_with = GER }
    NOT = { has_country_flag = FR3_R_agadir_requested }
    NOT = { has_global_flag = GE1_agadir_crisis_pending }
    NOT = { has_global_flag = GE1_agadir_settlement_resolved }
   }
   set_country_flag = FR3_R_agadir_requested
   country_event = { id = ww1_france.17 hours = 1 }
  }
}
}

US2_R_join_entente = {
 if = {
  limit = { US2_R_may_accept_alignment = yes NOT = { has_completed_focus = US2_R_german_alignment } NOT = { has_completed_focus = US2_neutrality } has_war_with = GER is_in_faction = no
 has_completed_focus = US2_declare_war_on_germany
}
  if = {
   limit = { country_exists = FRA FRA = { has_war_with = GER is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GER } FR3_R_may_enter_entente = yes } }
   FRA = { faction_leader = { add_to_faction = USA } }
  }
  else_if = {
   limit = { country_exists = ENG ENG = { has_war_with = GER is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GER } NOT = { has_country_flag = EN1_R_neutral_policy } } }
   ENG = { faction_leader = { add_to_faction = USA } }
  }
 }
}
US2_R_join_entente_war = {
 if = {
  limit = { US2_R_may_enter_entente_war = yes NOT = { has_war_with = GER }
 has_completed_focus = US2_declare_war_on_germany

 OR = { NOT = { WW1_historical_ai_preparation = yes } NOT = { date < 1917.4.6 } }
}
  declare_war_on = { target = GER type = annex_everything }
  set_country_flag = US2_R_war_authorized set_global_flag = USA_joins_entente
  clr_global_flag = USA_stays_neutral clr_global_flag = USA_neutrality_focus
  US2_R_join_entente = yes
 }
}
US2_R_adopt_neutrality = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = USA US2_R_no_great_war = yes }
  if = {
   limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
   random_other_country = { limit = { is_in_faction_with = USA NOT = { tag = USA } is_subject = no } set_faction_leader = yes }
  }
  if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes }
  else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
  set_country_flag = US2_R_enduring_neutrality set_global_flag = USA_stays_neutral
  set_global_flag = USA_neutrality_focus clr_global_flag = USA_joins_entente
  clr_global_flag = USA_support_entente clr_country_flag = US2_R_entente_policy
  add_ideas = USA_isolationism
 }
}
US2_R_request_german_alignment = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = USA US2_R_no_great_war = yes country_exists = GER is_subject = no NOT = { has_country_flag = US2_R_german_offer_pending } NOT = { has_country_flag = US2_R_german_offer_resolved } }
  set_country_flag = US2_R_german_offer_pending
  GER = { country_event = { id = ww1_usa.209 days = 1 } }
 }
}
US2_R_request_continental_alignment = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = USA US2_R_no_great_war = yes country_exists = FRA has_global_flag = GE1_anglo_german_alliance NOT = { has_country_flag = US2_R_continental_offer_pending } NOT = { has_country_flag = US2_R_continental_offer_resolved } }
  set_country_flag = US2_R_continental_offer_pending
  FRA = { country_event = { id = ww1_usa.212 days = 1 } }
 }
}
US2_R_join_german_bloc = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = USA US2_R_no_great_war = yes has_country_flag = US2_R_german_agreement GER = { is_in_faction = yes } is_in_faction = no
 has_completed_focus = US2_R_ratify_german_alignment
}
  GER = { faction_leader = { add_to_faction = USA } }
  set_country_flag = US2_R_german_bloc_member news_event = { id = ww1_usa.224 hours = 6 }
  if = { limit = { GER = { has_war_with = FRA } country_exists = FRA NOT = { is_in_faction_with = FRA } } declare_war_on = { target = FRA type = annex_everything } }
  if = { limit = { has_global_flag = GE1_anglo_german_alliance GER = { has_war_with = RIE } country_exists = RIE NOT = { is_in_faction_with = RIE } } declare_war_on = { target = RIE type = annex_everything } }
 }
}
US2_R_join_continental_bloc = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = USA US2_R_no_great_war = yes has_country_flag = US2_R_continental_agreement has_global_flag = GE1_continental_alliance_active FRA = { is_in_faction = yes has_war_with = GER } is_in_faction = no
 has_completed_focus = US2_R_ratify_continental_alignment
}
  FRA = { faction_leader = { add_to_faction = USA } }
  declare_war_on = { target = GER type = annex_everything }
  if = { limit = { FRA = { has_war_with = ENG } country_exists = ENG NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ENG } } declare_war_on = { target = ENG type = annex_everything } }
  set_country_flag = US2_R_continental_bloc_member news_event = { id = ww1_usa.225 hours = 6 }
 }
}
US2_R_promote_elected_president = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = USA NOT = { date < 1913.3.4 } date < 1921.3.4 NOT = { has_country_flag = US2_R_inaugurated } }
  if = {
   limit = { has_country_flag = US2_R_roosevelt_elected }
   endsieg_character_activate_us2_r_theodore_roosevelt_b605402e = yes
   US2_R_theodore_roosevelt = { promote_character = yes }
  }
  else_if = {
   limit = { has_country_flag = US2_R_taft_elected }
   USA_William_H_Taft = { promote_character = yes }
  }
  else = {
   endsieg_character_activate_usa_woodrow_wilson_794d791a = yes
   USA_woodrow_wilson = { promote_character = yes }
   set_country_flag = US2_R_wilson_elected
  }
  set_country_flag = US2_R_inaugurated
 }
}
US2_R_initialize_bookmark = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = USA has_start_date < 1936.1.1 NOT = { has_country_flag = US2_R_bookmark_initialized } }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { date < 1913.3.4 } } set_country_flag = US2_R_wilson_elected set_country_flag = US2_R_inaugurated set_country_flag = US2_R_reserve_act }
  if = { limit = { date > 1914.11.22 } set_global_flag = veracruz_withdrawn }
  if = { limit = { date > 1915.7.27 } set_country_flag = US2_R_haiti_settled }
  if = { limit = { date > 1916.3.8 } set_global_flag = columbus_raid }
  if = { limit = { date > 1916.5.4 } set_country_flag = US2_R_dominican_settled }
  if = { limit = { date > 1916.7.29 } set_global_flag = black_tom }
  if = { limit = { date > 1914.8.14 } set_country_flag = US2_R_canal_opened }
  if = { limit = { date > 1913.12.22 } set_country_flag = US2_R_federal_reserve_announced }
  if = {
   limit = { NOT = { has_start_date < 1917.4.6 } date < 1919.1.1 country_exists = GER }
   set_country_flag = US2_R_entente_policy
   US2_R_join_entente_war = yes
   if = { limit = { NOT = { date < 1917.12.7 } country_exists = AUH AUH = { is_in_faction_with = GER } NOT = { has_war_with = AUH } } declare_war_on = { target = AUH type = annex_everything } }
   set_global_flag = USA_sends_AEF set_country_flag = US2_R_ae_french_liaison
   set_country_flag = US2_R_war_vote_announced set_global_flag = USA_zimmerman_condemned set_global_flag = USA_demands_USW_stop set_country_flag = US2_R_fourteen_points
  }
  set_country_flag = US2_R_bookmark_initialized

 if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = US2_support_entente } set_country_flag = US2_R_entente_policy set_global_flag = USA_support_entente }
 if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = US2_condemn_unrestricted_sub } set_global_flag = USA_demands_USW_stop }
 if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = US2_condemn_zimmerman_telegram } set_global_flag = USA_zimmerman_condemned }
 if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = US2_send_expeditionary_force } set_global_flag = USA_sends_AEF }
}
}
US2_R_update_tree = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = USA has_start_date < 1936.1.1 }
  US2_R_initialize_bookmark = yes
  if = {
   limit = { has_focus_tree = usa_ww1_focus date < 1936.1.1 }
   if = { limit = { NOT = { date < 1913.3.4 } date < 1913.3.11 has_start_date < 1913.3.4 NOT = { has_country_flag = US2_R_inaugurated } } country_event = { id = ww1_usa.201 hours = 1 } }
   if = { limit = { has_war_with = GER US2_R_may_accept_alignment = yes is_in_faction = no has_country_flag = US2_R_war_authorized } US2_R_join_entente = yes }
   if = {
    limit = { is_historical_focus_on = yes US2_R_may_enter_entente_war = yes has_start_date < 1917.4.6 NOT = { date < 1917.4.6 } date < 1917.4.13 NOT = { has_country_flag = US2_R_war_vote_announced } OR = { has_global_flag = GE1_resumes_USW has_global_flag = USA_zimmerman_condemned } }
    set_country_flag = US2_R_war_vote_announced country_event = { id = ww1_usa.8 hours = 1 }
   }
   if = { limit = { is_historical_focus_on = yes has_war_with = GER has_start_date < 1917.12.7 NOT = { date < 1917.12.7 } date < 1917.12.14 country_exists = AUH AUH = { is_in_faction_with = GER has_war_with = FRA } NOT = { has_war_with = AUH } NOT = { has_country_flag = US2_R_auh_vote_announced } } set_country_flag = US2_R_auh_vote_announced country_event = { id = ww1_usa.215 hours = 1 } }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_focus_tree = usa_ww1_focus has_start_date < 1916.11.7 NOT = { date < 1916.11.7 } date < 1916.11.14 US2_R_historical_presidency = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = US2_R_1916_election_announced } } set_country_flag = US2_R_1916_election_announced country_event = { id = ww1_usa.208 hours = 1 } }
  if = { limit = { has_focus_tree = usa_ww1_focus has_start_date < 1918.1.8 NOT = { date < 1918.1.8 } date < 1918.1.15 has_war_with = GER US2_R_historical_presidency = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = US2_R_fourteen_points } } country_event = { id = ww1_usa.205 hours = 1 } }
  if = { limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_focus_tree = usa_ww1_focus } date > 1935.12.31 } } US2_R_clear_all_programmes = yes }
  else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_global_flag = compiegne has_global_flag = central_powers_victory } } US2_R_clear_war_programmes = yes }
 }
}

OT1_R_request_german_alignment = {
 if = {
  limit = { GER = { GE1_ww1_may_choose_central_bloc = yes } OT1_R_may_negotiate_alignment = yes country_exists = GER NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_entente_agreement } NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_german_offer_pending } NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_german_offer_cooldown }
 has_completed_focus = OT1_R_german_alignment OR = { NOT = { WW1_historical_ai_preparation = yes } NOT = { date < 1914.8.2 } }
}
  clr_country_flag = OT1_R_german_offer_accepted clr_country_flag = OT1_R_german_offer_refused set_country_flag = { flag = OT1_R_german_offer_pending days = 14 }
  GER = { country_event = { id = ww1_turkey.310 days = 1 } }
 }
}

 if = {
  limit = { OT1_R_may_negotiate_alignment = yes country_exists = ENG country_exists = FRA country_exists = RIE NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_german_agreement } NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_straits_offer_pending } NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_straits_offer_cooldown } }
  clr_country_flag = OT1_R_british_straits_consent clr_country_flag = OT1_R_russian_straits_consent clr_country_flag = OT1_R_french_straits_consent clr_country_flag = OT1_R_straits_refused set_country_flag = { flag = OT1_R_straits_offer_pending days = 14 }
  ENG = { country_event = { id = ww1_turkey.315 days = 1 } }
 }
}

  clr_country_flag = OT1_R_entente_offer_accepted clr_country_flag = OT1_R_entente_offer_refused set_country_flag = { flag = OT1_R_entente_offer_pending days = 14 }

OT1_R_join_central_war = {
 if = {
  limit = { GER = { GE1_ww1_may_choose_central_bloc = yes } tag = TUR OT1_R_may_follow_historical_alignment = yes has_country_flag = OT1_R_german_agreement country_exists = GER GER = { is_in_faction = yes } OT1_R_no_great_war = yes
 has_completed_focus = OT1_R_central_war

 has_completed_focus = TUR_strike_russian_port OR = { NOT = { WW1_historical_ai_preparation = yes } NOT = { date < 1914.11.3 } }
}
  if = { limit = { is_in_faction = no } GER = { faction_leader = { add_to_faction = TUR } } }
  if = {
   limit = { is_in_faction_with = GER }
   set_global_flag = TUR_joins_CP set_country_flag = OT1_R_central_war_authorized news_event = { id = ww1_turkey.346 hours = 6 }
   if = { limit = { country_exists = RIE GER = { has_war_with = RIE } RIE = { RUS_ww1_may_be_historical_war_target = yes } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = RIE } } declare_war_on = { target = RIE type = annex_everything } }
   if = { limit = { country_exists = SOV GER = { has_war_with = SOV } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = SOV } } declare_war_on = { target = SOV type = annex_everything } }
   if = { limit = { country_exists = ENG GER = { has_war_with = ENG } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ENG } } declare_war_on = { target = ENG type = annex_everything } }
   if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA GER = { has_war_with = FRA } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = FRA } } declare_war_on = { target = FRA type = annex_everything } }
  }
 }
}
OT1_R_join_entente_war = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = TUR has_country_flag = OT1_R_entente_agreement OT1_R_no_great_war = yes country_exists = GER country_exists = FRA FRA = { has_war_with = GER is_in_faction = yes FR3_R_may_enter_entente = yes } NOT = { has_non_aggression_pact_with = GER }
 has_completed_focus = OT1_R_entente_war
}
  if = { limit = { is_in_faction = no } FRA = { faction_leader = { add_to_faction = TUR } } }
  if = {
   limit = { is_in_faction_with = FRA }
   set_country_flag = OT1_R_entente_war_authorized news_event = { id = ww1_turkey.346 hours = 6 }
   declare_war_on = { target = GER type = annex_everything }
   if = { limit = { country_exists = AUH FRA = { has_war_with = AUH } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = AUH } } declare_war_on = { target = AUH type = annex_everything } }
  }
 }
}
OT1_R_adopt_neutrality = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = TUR OT1_R_no_great_war = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_german_agreement } NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_entente_agreement } }
  if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } random_other_country = { limit = { is_in_faction_with = TUR NOT = { tag = TUR } is_subject = no } set_faction_leader = yes } }
  if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes }
  else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
  set_country_flag = OT1_R_neutral_policy
 }
}

  clr_country_flag = OT1_R_italian_offer_accepted clr_country_flag = OT1_R_italian_offer_refused set_country_flag = { flag = OT1_R_italian_offer_pending days = 14 }

  clr_country_flag = OT1_R_balkan_BUL_accept clr_country_flag = OT1_R_balkan_SER_accept clr_country_flag = OT1_R_balkan_GRE_accept clr_country_flag = OT1_R_balkan_refused clr_country_flag = OT1_R_balkan_all_accept set_country_flag = { flag = OT1_R_balkan_offer_pending days = 14 }
  BUL = { country_event = { id = ww1_turkey.321 days = 1 } }
 }
