OT1_R_update_tree = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = TUR has_start_date < 1936.1.1 }
  if = {
   limit = { has_focus_tree = turkish_focus_ww1 NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_bookmark_initialized } }
   if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_pro_young_turk } OT1_R_choose_unionists = yes }
   if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_befriend_germany } set_country_flag = OT1_R_german_liaison }
   if = { limit = { has_start_date > 1914.10.28 is_in_faction_with = GER } set_country_flag = OT1_R_german_agreement set_global_flag = TUR_joins_CP }
   if = { limit = { has_start_date > 1913.1.22 } set_country_flag = OT1_R_coup_resolved }
   if = { limit = { has_start_date > 1913.6.11 date < 1917.2.4 } set_country_flag = OT1_R_said_halim_cabinet set_country_flag = OT1_R_said_halim_served add_ideas = OT1_R_said_halim_ministry }
   if = { limit = { has_start_date > 1917.2.3 } set_country_flag = OT1_R_talat_cabinet set_country_flag = OT1_R_said_halim_served add_ideas = OT1_R_talat_ministry }
   set_country_flag = OT1_R_bookmark_initialized
  }
  TUR_stab_daily = yes

  if = {
   limit = { has_focus_tree = turkish_focus_ww1 date < 1936.1.1 }
   if = { limit = { is_historical_focus_on = yes has_start_date < 1913.1.23 NOT = { date < 1913.1.23 } date < 1913.1.30 NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_alternative_government } NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_coup_resolved } } country_event = { id = ww1_turkey.35 hours = 1 } }

   if = { limit = { is_historical_focus_on = yes has_start_date < 1914.10.29 NOT = { date < 1914.10.29 } date < 1914.11.5 has_country_flag = OT1_R_german_agreement OT1_R_may_follow_historical_alignment = yes GER = { has_war_with = RIE } OT1_R_no_great_war = yes } country_event = { id = ww1_turkey.2 hours = 1 } }
   if = { limit = { has_start_date < 1918.7.3 NOT = { date < 1918.7.3 } date < 1918.7.10 has_country_leader = { ruling_only = yes character = TUR_mehmed_v } NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_succession_scheduled } } set_country_flag = OT1_R_succession_scheduled country_event = { id = ww1_turkey.60 hours = 1 } }
  }

  if = { limit = { has_focus_tree = turkish_focus_ww1 is_historical_focus_on = yes has_start_date < 1913.6.12 NOT = { date < 1913.6.12 } date < 1913.6.19 NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_alternative_government } NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_said_halim_served } } country_event = { id = ww1_turkey.301 hours = 1 } }
  if = { limit = { has_focus_tree = turkish_focus_ww1 is_historical_focus_on = yes has_start_date < 1917.2.4 NOT = { date < 1917.2.4 } date < 1917.2.11 has_country_flag = OT1_R_unionist_path OT1_R_has_great_war = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_talat_cabinet } } country_event = { id = ww1_turkey.302 hours = 1 } }


  # Army mitigation is calculated within the one regional refresh.
  if = { limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_focus_tree = turkish_focus_ww1 } date > 1935.12.31 } } OT1_R_clear_all_programmes = yes }
  else_if = { limit = { has_war = no } OT1_R_clear_war_programmes = yes }
 }
}

OT1_R_apply_mudros = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = TUR NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_mudros_resolved } OR = { has_war_with = ENG has_war_with = FRA } }
  set_country_flag = OT1_R_mudros_resolved clr_country_flag = OT1_R_armistice_pending
  set_global_flag = armistice_of_mudros set_cosmetic_tag = TUR_ottoman_empire
  news_event = { id = endsieg_news.2 hours = 6 }
  if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } random_other_country = { limit = { is_in_faction_with = TUR NOT = { tag = TUR } is_subject = no } set_faction_leader = yes } }
  if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes }
  else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
  every_other_country = { limit = { has_war_with = TUR } white_peace = TUR }
  if = { limit = { country_exists = EN3 EN3 = { is_subject_of = ENG } } ENG = { annex_country = { target = EN3 transfer_troops = no } } }
  if = { limit = { country_exists = ENG } ENG = { if = { limit = { 446 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 446 } if = { limit = { 453 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 453 } if = { limit = { 454 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 454 } if = { limit = { 987 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 987 } if = { limit = { 988 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 988 } if = { limit = { 675 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 675 } if = { limit = { 455 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 455 } if = { limit = { 291 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 291 } if = { limit = { 676 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 676 } if = { limit = { 656 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 656 } if = { limit = { 293 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 293 } if = { limit = { 679 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 679 } if = { limit = { 765 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 765 } if = { limit = { 855 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 855 } if = { limit = { 856 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 856 } } }
if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA } FRA = { if = { limit = { 680 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 680 } if = { limit = { 677 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 677 } if = { limit = { 553 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 553 } if = { limit = { 554 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 554 } if = { limit = { 799 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 799 } if = { limit = { 1032 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 1032 } if = { limit = { 345 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 345 } if = { limit = { 344 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 344 } if = { limit = { 350 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 350 } } }
if = { limit = { country_exists = ITA } ITA = { if = { limit = { 342 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 342 } } }
  if = { limit = { country_exists = SHA } SHA = { country_event = { id = shammar.2 days = 1 } } }
  if = { limit = { owns_state = 1033 controls_state = 1033 NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } if = { limit = { country_exists = ARM } ARM = { transfer_state = 1033 } } else_if = { limit = { country_exists = GEO } GEO = { transfer_state = 1033 } } else = { TRC = { add_state_core = 1033 transfer_state = 1033 } } }
  if = { limit = { 797 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } 797 = { set_demilitarized_zone = yes } }
  if = { limit = { 341 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } 341 = { set_demilitarized_zone = yes } }
  if = { limit = { has_idea = sick_man_europe } remove_ideas = sick_man_europe }
  if = { limit = { has_idea = fall_of_russia } remove_ideas = fall_of_russia }
  if = { limit = { has_idea = turkish_german_training } remove_ideas = turkish_german_training }
  if = { limit = { has_idea = desertion } remove_ideas = desertion }
  if = { limit = { has_idea = jihad } remove_ideas = jihad }
  OT1_R_clear_all_programmes = yes OT1_R_end_administration = yes
 }
}

OT1_R_apply_balkan_settlement = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = TUR has_country_flag = OT1_R_balkan_all_accept NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_balkan_settlement } NOT = { has_war_with = BUL } NOT = { has_war_with = GRE } NOT = { has_war_with = SER } }
  set_country_flag = OT1_R_balkan_settlement set_global_flag = let_go_balkans
  BUL = { if = { limit = { 1036 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 1036 } if = { limit = { 1063 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 1063 } if = { limit = { 997 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 997 } if = { limit = { 184 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 184 } if = { limit = { 1021 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 1021 } }
GRE = { if = { limit = { 1062 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 1062 } if = { limit = { 973 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 973 } if = { limit = { 731 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 731 } if = { limit = { 805 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 805 } if = { limit = { 1042 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 1042 } if = { limit = { 1024 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 1024 } }
MNT = { if = { limit = { 1037 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 1037 } }
SER = { if = { limit = { 802 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 802 } if = { limit = { 106 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 106 } if = { limit = { 1038 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 1038 } if = { limit = { 934 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } transfer_state = 934 } }
ALB = { if = { limit = { 44 = { is_owned_by = TUR } } add_state_core = 44 transfer_state = 44 } }
  diplomatic_relation = { country = BUL relation = non_aggression_pact active = yes }
  diplomatic_relation = { country = GRE relation = non_aggression_pact active = yes }
  diplomatic_relation = { country = SER relation = non_aggression_pact active = yes }
  if = { limit = { country_exists = MNT } diplomatic_relation = { country = MNT relation = non_aggression_pact active = yes } }
  news_event = { id = ww1_turkey.347 hours = 6 }
 }
}

# Ottoman WW1 administration helpers.
# Integrate this file as common/scripted_effects/OT1_R_stability.txt.
# OT1_R_administration_allowed, OT1_R_no_great_war and OT1_R_has_great_war
# are scripted triggers supplied by the root integration.

OT1_R_clamp_administration = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = OT1_R_cabinet_authority } } set_variable = { OT1_R_cabinet_authority = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = OT1_R_provincial_confidence } } set_variable = { OT1_R_provincial_confidence = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = OT1_R_officer_loyalty } } set_variable = { OT1_R_officer_loyalty = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = OT1_R_reform_capacity } } set_variable = { OT1_R_reform_capacity = 0 } }
	clamp_variable = { var = OT1_R_cabinet_authority min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = OT1_R_provincial_confidence min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = OT1_R_officer_loyalty min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = OT1_R_reform_capacity min = 0 max = 3 }
}

OT1_R_refresh_administration = {
 TUR_stab_import_provincial_delta = yes OT1_R_clamp_administration = yes
}

OT1_R_initialize_administration = {
 TUR_stab_ensure_initialized = yes
}

OT1_R_monthly_administration = {
 # Compatibility API; the regional daily owner is the only monthly scheduler.
 TUR_stab_ensure_initialized = yes
}

OT1_R_check_administration_crises = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OT1_R_administration_allowed = yes
			has_country_flag = OT1_R_administration_active
			has_country_flag = OT1_R_administration_initialized
			NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_crisis_active }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_cabinet_crisis_cooldown }
			check_variable = { OT1_R_cabinet_authority < 20 }
		}
		set_country_flag = OT1_R_crisis_active
		set_country_flag = OT1_R_cabinet_crisis_active
		activate_mission = OT1_R_crisis_cabinet
		country_event = { id = ww1_turkey.330 days = 1 }
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			OT1_R_administration_allowed = yes
			has_country_flag = OT1_R_administration_active
			has_country_flag = OT1_R_administration_initialized
			NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_crisis_active }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = OT1_R_officer_crisis_cooldown }
			check_variable = { OT1_R_officer_loyalty < 20 }
		}
		set_country_flag = OT1_R_crisis_active
		set_country_flag = OT1_R_officer_crisis_active
		activate_mission = OT1_R_crisis_officers
		country_event = { id = ww1_turkey.332 days = 1 }
	}
}

# visible 30-60 day duration.

	clr_country_flag = OT1_R_crisis_active

	clr_country_flag = OT1_R_provincial_crisis_active

	clr_country_flag = OT1_R_provincial_crisis_cooldown
	clr_country_flag = OT1_R_officer_crisis_cooldown
	remove_mission = OT1_R_crisis_cabinet
	remove_mission = OT1_R_crisis_province
	remove_mission = OT1_R_crisis_officers

	remove_mission = OT1_R_301_provincial_consultation_mission
	remove_mission = OT1_R_302_staff_mediation_mission

	remove_mission = OT1_R_305_officer_rotation_mission

JP1_R_add_wartime_arms = {
 if = { limit = { JP1_R_has_major_war = yes }
  random_owned_controlled_state = { limit = { is_core_of = JAP NOT = { has_state_flag = endsieg_ww1_industry_demobilized } } set_temp_variable = { endsieg_ww1_factories_to_add = 1 } endsieg_ww1_expand_state_war_industry = yes }
 }
}
JP1_R_request_british_alignment = {
 if = { limit = { JP1_R_may_negotiate = yes country_exists = ENG NOT = { has_war_with = ENG } NOT = { has_country_flag = JP1_R_british_offer_pending } NOT = { has_country_flag = JP1_R_british_offer_cooldown } }
  set_country_flag = { flag = JP1_R_british_offer_pending days = 30 }
  ENG = { country_event = { id = ww1_japan.310 days = 1 } }
 }
}
JP1_R_request_german_alignment = {
 if = { limit = { JP1_R_may_negotiate = yes country_exists = GER NOT = { has_war_with = GER } NOT = { has_country_flag = JP1_R_german_offer_pending } NOT = { has_country_flag = JP1_R_german_offer_cooldown } }
  set_country_flag = { flag = JP1_R_german_offer_pending days = 30 }
  GER = { country_event = { id = ww1_japan.312 days = 1 } }
 }
}
JP1_R_detach_from_faction = {
 if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } random_other_country = { limit = { is_in_faction_with = JAP NOT = { tag = JAP } is_subject = no } set_faction_leader = yes } }
 if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes }
 else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
}
JP1_R_form_asian_faction = {
 if = { limit = { JP1_R_may_negotiate = yes has_war = no }
  JP1_R_detach_from_faction = yes
  set_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
  create_faction_from_template = faction_template_JP1_R_east_asian_league
  set_country_flag = JP1_R_asian_alignment
  set_country_flag = JP1_R_independent_strategy
  news_event = { id = ww1_japan.352 hours = 6 }
 }
}
JP1_R_adopt_neutrality = {
 if = { limit = { JP1_R_may_negotiate = yes has_war = no }
  JP1_R_detach_from_faction = yes set_country_flag = JP1_R_neutrality
 }
}
JP1_R_join_entente = {
 if = { limit = { JP1_R_ww1_allowed = yes has_country_flag = JP1_R_entente_alignment country_exists = ENG ENG = { is_in_faction = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = ENG }
 has_completed_focus = JP1_R_issue_german_ultimatum_decision

 ENG = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GER } } OR = { NOT = { WW1_historical_ai_preparation = yes } NOT = { date < 1914.8.23 } }
}
  if = { limit = { is_in_faction = no } ENG = { faction_leader = { add_to_faction = JAP } } }
 }
}
JP1_R_join_german_alliance = {
 if = { limit = { JP1_R_ww1_allowed = yes has_country_flag = JP1_R_german_alignment country_exists = GER GER = { is_in_faction = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = GER }
 has_completed_focus = JP1_R_commit_german_alignment
}
  if = { limit = { is_in_faction = no } GER = { faction_leader = { add_to_faction = JAP } } }
 }
}
JP1_R_issue_german_ultimatum = {
 if = { limit = { JP1_R_may_follow_historical_alignment = yes has_country_flag = JP1_R_entente_alignment country_exists = GER country_exists = ENG ENG = { has_war_with = GER } NOT = { has_war_with = GER } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GER } NOT = { has_non_aggression_pact_with = GER } NOT = { has_country_flag = JP1_R_ultimatum_sent }
 has_completed_focus = JP1_R_issue_german_ultimatum_decision OR = { NOT = { WW1_historical_ai_preparation = yes } NOT = { date < 1914.8.15 } }
}
  set_country_flag = JP1_R_ultimatum_sent country_event = { id = ww1_japan.1 days = 1 }
 }
}
JP1_R_prepare_russian_campaign = {
 if = { limit = { JP1_R_may_attack_russia = yes } set_country_flag = JP1_R_russian_campaign JP1_R_target_408 = yes JP1_R_target_655 = yes }
}
JP1_R_invite_siam = {
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = JP1_R_siam_offer_cooldown }  JP1_R_ww1_allowed = yes has_country_flag = JP1_R_asian_alignment is_faction_leader = yes country_exists = SIA NOT = { has_war_with = SIA } SIA = { is_subject = no is_in_faction = no } NOT = { has_country_flag = JP1_R_siam_offer_pending } }
  set_country_flag = { flag = JP1_R_siam_offer_pending days = 30 } SIA = { country_event = { id = ww1_japan.314 days = 1 } }
 }
}

JP1_R_enter_german_war = {
 if = { limit = { JP1_R_may_follow_historical_alignment = yes has_country_flag = JP1_R_german_war_approved NOT = { date < 1914.8.23 } country_exists = GER NOT = { has_war_with = GER } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = GER } NOT = { has_non_aggression_pact_with = GER } }
  JP1_R_join_entente = yes declare_war_on = { target = GER type = annex_everything }
 }
}
JP1_R_request_chinese_concessions = {

  608 = { owner = { if = { limit = { OR = { tag = CHI tag = QIN tag = RCH tag = PRC } NOT = { has_war_with = JAP } NOT = { is_subject_of = JAP } } JAP = { set_country_flag = { flag = JP1_R_chinese_offer_pending days = 30 } } country_event = { id = ww1_japan.320 days = 1 } } } }
 }
}

JP1_R_accept_russian_settlement = {
 if = { limit = { has_war_with = JAP JAP = { controls_state = 408 controls_state = 409 controls_state = 655 } }
  save_event_target_as = JP1_R_russian_settlement_country
  JAP = {
   if = { limit = { 408 = { is_owned_by = event_target:JP1_R_russian_settlement_country is_controlled_by = JAP } } transfer_state = 408 }
   if = { limit = { 409 = { is_owned_by = event_target:JP1_R_russian_settlement_country is_controlled_by = JAP } } transfer_state = 409 }
   if = { limit = { 655 = { is_owned_by = event_target:JP1_R_russian_settlement_country is_controlled_by = JAP } } transfer_state = 655 }
   white_peace = event_target:JP1_R_russian_settlement_country
   clr_country_flag = JP1_R_russian_peace_pending set_country_flag = JP1_R_far_eastern_settlement
  }
 }
}
JP1_R_create_far_eastern_buffer = {
 if = { limit = { JP1_R_ww1_allowed = yes has_country_flag = JP1_R_far_eastern_settlement owns_state = 408 owns_state = 409 country_exists = VLA VLA = { is_subject_of = JAP } } VLA = { transfer_state = 408 transfer_state = 409 } set_country_flag = JP1_R_buffer_formed }
 else_if = { limit = { JP1_R_ww1_allowed = yes has_country_flag = JP1_R_far_eastern_settlement owns_state = 408 owns_state = 409 NOT = { country_exists = VLA } }
  # Release uses existing VLA cores. Keep every Japanese-owned district outside
  # the two treaty provinces, including northern Sakhalin, in Japanese hands.
  every_owned_state = { limit = { is_core_of = VLA NOT = { state = 408 } NOT = { state = 409 } } set_state_flag = JP1_R_retain_after_buffer }
  release_puppet = VLA
  every_state = { limit = { has_state_flag = JP1_R_retain_after_buffer } JAP = { transfer_state = PREV } clr_state_flag = JP1_R_retain_after_buffer }
  if = { limit = { country_exists = VLA VLA = { is_subject_of = JAP } } set_country_flag = JP1_R_buffer_formed }
 }
}

JP1_R_update_tree = {
 if = { limit = { tag = JAP has_start_date < 1936.1.1 }
  if = { limit = { date < 1936.1.1 has_focus_tree = JAP_ww1_focus NOT = { has_country_flag = JP1_R_bookmark_initialized } }
   if = { limit = { has_start_date > 1914.8.22 } set_country_flag = JP1_R_entente_alignment set_country_flag = JP1_R_ultimatum_sent set_country_flag = JP1_R_german_war_approved JP1_R_join_entente = yes JP1_R_enter_german_war = yes }
   if = { limit = { has_start_date > 1912.7.29 } set_global_flag = meiji_dead }
   if = { limit = { has_start_date < 1912.7.30 has_character = JAP_emperor_meiji } promote_character = JAP_emperor_meiji }
   if = { limit = { has_start_date > 1912.9.12 has_character = JAP_nogi_maresuke } retire_character = JAP_nogi_maresuke }
   if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = JP1_R_elder_statesmen } set_country_flag = JP1_R_historical_cabinet }
   set_country_flag = JP1_R_bookmark_initialized
  }
  if = { limit = { has_focus_tree = JAP_ww1_focus date < 1936.1.1 }
   if = { limit = { is_historical_focus_on = yes has_start_date < 1914.8.15 NOT = { date < 1914.8.15 } date < 1914.8.22 JP1_R_may_follow_historical_alignment = yes country_exists = ENG ENG = { has_war_with = GER } NOT = { has_country_flag = JP1_R_entente_alignment } } JP1_R_request_british_alignment = yes }
   if = { limit = { is_ai = yes is_historical_focus_on = yes has_start_date < 1914.8.15 NOT = { date < 1914.8.15 } date < 1914.8.29 JP1_R_may_negotiate = yes has_country_flag = JP1_R_british_agreement } set_country_flag = JP1_R_entente_alignment }
   if = { limit = { is_historical_focus_on = yes has_start_date < 1914.8.15 NOT = { date < 1914.8.15 } date < 1914.8.29 } JP1_R_issue_german_ultimatum = yes }
   JP1_R_enter_german_war = yes
   if = { limit = { is_historical_focus_on = yes has_start_date < 1915.1.18 NOT = { date < 1915.1.18 } date < 1915.1.25 has_country_flag = JP1_R_entente_alignment } JP1_R_request_chinese_concessions = yes }
   if = { limit = { is_historical_focus_on = yes has_start_date < 1918.8.2 NOT = { date < 1918.8.2 } date < 1918.8.9 has_country_flag = JP1_R_entente_alignment } JP1_R_request_siberian_intervention = yes }
   if = { limit = { is_historical_focus_on = yes has_start_date < 1918.9.29 NOT = { date < 1918.9.29 } date < 1918.10.6 has_country_flag = JP1_R_historical_cabinet NOT = { has_country_flag = JP1_R_hara_ministry } } country_event = { id = ww1_japan.307 hours = 1 } }
   if = { limit = { has_country_flag = JP1_R_chinese_concessions NOT = { has_completed_focus = JP1_R_request_chinese_concessions_negotiation } } complete_national_focus = JP1_R_request_chinese_concessions_negotiation }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_focus_tree = JAP_ww1_focus date > 1935.12.31 } JP1_R_clear_programmes = yes load_focus_tree = { tree = japan_wtt_focus keep_completed = yes } }
  else_if = { limit = { NOT = { has_focus_tree = JAP_ww1_focus } } JP1_R_clear_programmes = yes }
 }
}
